## BLOG

## Friday, August 26: 12:03 pm - Canadian Meat Products

There were five last chances for players to grind into Nationals last night. With four players from each event getting to play today it meant almost a full third of the field came from the Grinders. Here are the decklists from the first four events last night. As soon as I can track down the decks from the fifth event I will get them in as well - most likely tomorrow morning.

This is the first look at Ninth Edition Standard and it is not much of a surprise the big hits early on seem to be [Llanowar Wastes](https://web.archive.org/web/20050827000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Llanowar+Wastes) and [Hypnotic Specter](https://web.archive.org/web/20050827000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hypnotic+Specter). Almost every grinder featured at least one player qualifying with a Rat deck that touched green for [Viridian Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20050827000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Viridian+Shaman) and featured some number of [Hippie](https://web.archive.org/web/20050827000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hippie)s. There was one deck that went a little further and harkened back to Extended Rock builds. The only other intriguing card from Ninth that stood out was a couple of copies of [Tidings](https://web.archive.org/web/20050827000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tidings) in BlueTooth.
